This source export does not include historical videos. Historical evidence links lead to the handoff notes. New results are stored in outputs/ after evaluation.

ROBOTWORLD / EVALUATION NOTES评分解释 · 源码核对版 · 2026.09.29
先解释分数，再比较模型

这道题，到底怎么算分？

把“整道题有没有完成”和“过程中做得多好”分开讲清。涵盖全部选题，不只这次 smoke 跑过的题。16项World状态式判定已实现并验证接线；物理可行性待校准；移除接触异常退让题。

先回答你问的那串标签

“连续回报/误差，无整题 SR；整题 SR；World 整题 SR”原来列在 WheeledLab 整个 benchmark 后面，表示它的不同题采用三种评分方式。不是一道题有三项分数要相加，也不是三个高低等级。

原生漂移看回报；高差地形有到达目标判据；我们增加的倒车入库等题，用 World 自定义的考试规则判通过。下面按每道题分别解释。

已认可场景设计 · 16项现行逐题评分 · 84题评分概念解释
STATE CHECKS / WORLD STATE V1

环境负责出题，success只检查状态

已实现 · 接线已验证 · 可行性待校准

16个场景，27条状态检查。每题1～2条；力、时刻、场地和预算归环境设置。原生失败单独保留，原连续分继续报告。

最终状态 · final

结束时是否完成。例如路线已走完、达标击球数≥4。

连续保持 · hold

最后N秒一直满足。例如最后2秒在目标区停稳；中途违例不能拼接好帧。

全程状态 · always

指定窗口内一直满足。例如运输全程不掉物、完整轨迹窗口内跟踪达标。

统一裁定：有效运行中：没有原生/已声明场景失败，并且本题所有状态检查为true，才为World success。环境设置不是额外success条款。
过程要求保留为轨迹状态；只看最后一帧无法证明之前有没有掉物、跳跃或完成路线。

统一执行约定与统计方式
  • 通常评测时刻T为固定模拟时长；乒乓按5个正式球结束。不能提前截断考程避开后续扰动。
  • 资产/传感器/施力器故障等单列invalid，success=null；原生任务失败计0，不剔除。模型在后续扰动前已失败仍是有效失败。
  • 过程压缩为评测端状态（路线完成/真实跳跃计数等），按完整轨迹更新；重置、瞬移或模型自报不能改变它。
  • 重置与每个控制步读取真实状态，以模拟时间评估；未逐物理子步采样，不能宣称控制步之间也连续满足。视频抽帧不参与计时。接触使用各bench声明的传感器阈值。
  • 保留原状态/度量源码依据；新增容差、场地、保持时长标明World。正反例和状态读取已通过，完整物理成功轨迹与难度校准仍待验证。
  • 接触异常退让题已按用户要求移除，不进入当前题集与总成功率。
  • World SR按有效回合的布尔结果统计；来源无官方SR时仍称World SR。checker已接入；本次没有运行GPT评测，诊断不计模型SR。

已按本次指令记录设计认可；下方可继续标注修订意见。checker已实现；状态测试通过不等于已获得物理成功轨迹。

Go2 Push Recovery / quadruped_push_recovery

Go2受扰行走，到点停稳

1条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（8步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让Go2在平地上按给定的前进、侧移与转向速度行走，并在受推时维持或恢复步态；原任务没有指定目的地。

新场景：沿通道走到2.5m外的目标区，在受扰后站稳。

环境设置

  • 平地；目标在初始朝向前方2.5m，通道宽1.2m。
  • 6s、14s各施加30N水平力脉冲，持续0.2s；方向由固定seed决定。
  • 越出通道为World场景失败；沿用原非法躯干接触终止。

预算：1000步 / 50Hz ≈ 20s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 连续保持 · 最后 2 秒

    在目标区内、低速且直立，最后连续保持2秒。

    goal_distance <= 0.25 m AND speed <= 0.10 m/s AND tilt <= 20 deg
状态怎么计算？
  • goal_distance：root到目标中心的水平距离（m）；speed：root水平速度（m/s）；tilt：身体偏离正常直立的角度。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：删除逐次恢复限时及冲量90%条款；施力参数和越界规则归环境，成功只看末段到点停稳。

展开原判定映射、源码依据与prompt对照

原状态或奖励有依据，不代表新增容差和时长已经验证；所有新阈值仍需校准。

  1. S01 · 在目标区内、低速且直立，最后连续保持2秒。

    原任务依据是行走/姿态跟踪；终点容差和2秒为World新增。

历史运行快照原文（不是本次新运行；也不保证等于随后审计过的最新prompt）

原文件
You are the robot's policy, running through the locally source-built Codex app-server.
Use only dynamic robot tools to control the local simulator. No reset, teleportation, scene/physics edits, private evaluator queries or file/network bypass. Shell is for calculations in /workspace. Only allowlisted observations are in /observations. No pretrained task controller is supplied unless the project explicitly declares one.
apply_action executes the whole native action vector simultaneously, with its original scaling, actuators and controller. A completed tool call is not task success. observe costs no physics steps. The step budget counts native env.step calls, not tokens, physics substeps or tool calls. Physics pauses during LLM reasoning, shell computation and between callbacks: this is NOT a measurement of real-time inference latency.
Use short action segments or coding_control for fast feedback. Define control(obs,memory), returning {"action":[values]} or {"done":true} to return to reasoning without another physics step. JSON memory persists within one call. Arithmetic, math, loops, conditionals and lists/dicts are available; no NumPy, arbitrary imports, simulator handles or file access. Limits: 16000 source characters, 100000 operations, 4096 collection elements, 2s per callback. Invalid code takes no physics step unless earlier ticks of that call already executed.
Only the original actor policy group is supplied. It may include simulator-derived state, commands or a task's native predictor; consult this task's explicit observation contract. Never claim pure visual control if state is included. Critic/privileged evaluator observations are excluded. Third-person review video is never a policy input. Native sensor images, if any, are labelled separately.
At tool boundaries you receive current plus at most four historical observations sampled every two tool observations. Each includes its actual control_step; history does NOT mean every second physical step. Callback feedback is current at every control step. Review videos record every control step independently of prompt history.
Continue attempting until native termination or the allowed budget. A final text answer does not stop an active episode. Preserve the original task objective; do not optimize an invented success rule or call survival success where upstream reports only rewards/metrics.

Control Unitree Go2 full-body joint targets to follow the original sampled body velocity/yaw commands and reject native random force pulses. No pretrained gait or whole-body stabilizer is provided: only native DC motor PD tracking.
Native action is12 joint-position residuals: target=default_joint_position+0.25*u radians. The actual articulation joint order is exported in action metadata; use that exact order rather than assuming per-leg order. Default hips: left+0.1/right-0.1rad, front thighs0.8/rear thighs1.0, calves-1.5. Motor stiffness25, damping0.5, saturation23.5Nm. Native term has no configured action clipping; physical limits still apply. Do not confuse a nominal normalized value with a guaranteed safe joint range.
Native policy terms are body linear velocity(3), angular velocity(3), projected gravity(3), velocity_commands(3), joint position relative to default(12), joint velocity(12), last action(12):48values with original observation noise/scaling. No critic data or terrain height scan is added. API delivers named native_policy_terms; inspect metadata and names. Native state observation, not claimed pure visual.
One control tick=.02s, four physics substeps=.005s; horizon20s/1000ticks. Preserve the sampled commands, reset randomization and original noisy actor input. A code-control function can provide per-tick feedback from the delivered actor terms; it cannot read the environment or hidden force state directly.
Random impulse triggers every6-10s with requested duration0.15-0.25s; original event checks every0.18-0.22s, so actual force expiry is quantized to checks. Impulse max_force starts30N, not120N. Curriculum reaches120N after19200common steps; do not pretend a fresh single episode is the trained end curriculum. Sustained loads are scheduled25-40s apart, requested8-12s duration, start10N and rise later; these may not occur in a20s episode. Do not claim sustained-load recovery if none triggered. This is not the author's scheduled120N demo.
Report native reward/velocity tracking, base-contact termination, sampled-force curriculum state and actual trigger counts. Surviving the horizon alone is not a task success rate. Continue attempting control until a native termination or budget limit. Third-person review video does not grant privileged policy observations.
PUBLIC OPERATING BRIEF (shared RoboDojo-style information structure)
Scene and surroundings: Unitree Go2 follows sampled body velocity/yaw commands on native flat ground, with native force disturbances when their event schedule triggers.
Robot and interaction geometry: Four legs and 12 joint targets support a floating body. Lifting feet changes support; the motor PD only follows joint angles and cannot guarantee balance. Match the runtime joint ordering, joint signs and observation scaling before coordinating a gait.
Frames, units and control strength: the task-specific instructions, exact runtime action map and tool schema below/above are authoritative. Never transfer another benchmark's gripper polarity, quaternion order, normalized gain or world/root frame. A command range is not a collision-free or balance-safe region.
Available evidence: distinguish public model constants from current measured state and from unknown quantities. Exact object sizes, poses, contact forces, mass/friction randomization and hidden locations are unknown unless explicitly supplied by the allowed observation contract. Infer visible geometry with uncertainty; do not invent measurements or read simulator assets for answers.
Feedback and contact: reason about the complete moving robot and any carried object, not just a target point. Use measured response to check signs and scale, shorten segments near contact or instability, and reassess after errors. A joint servo or IK solver is not automatically a balance controller or collision-free planner. Use only tools actually listed; a coding tool, when listed, returns the same native actions and gains no extra sensors.
Completion and termination: follow the original objective and evaluator. Successful tool execution is not task success. Continue while active, but native failure and timeout remain terminal; no recovery override, reset or extension is authorized. Observation history samples tool feedback, while review video records control steps independently. Physics is paused during model deliberation.
ACTION CONTRACT — T10
Use exactly 12 finite scalars in action[0] through action[11], in the order below. All channels act simultaneously.
One control step = 0.02 s. Holding steps=10 lasts 0.2 simulated seconds; steps=50 lasts 1 s, unless terminated earlier.
apply_action repeats the SAME command for its steps; it does not multiply its amplitude or interpolate a trajectory. A repeated position target stays absolute relative to its stated reference; an increment interface explicitly accumulates.
Magnitude examples below are unit conversions, NOT recommended motions, safe ranges or measured gains. Input 1 is not universally maximum strength. Input limits, processed-target clipping and physical actuator limits are different.
Positive joint commands increase that named joint coordinate along its authored axis. Left/right mirrored joints can have different geometric effects; do not assume positive means forward/up for every joint.
Commanded targets are not guaranteed achieved positions/velocities or direct torque/force commands. Native servos, inertia, contacts, saturation and configured delays remain active. Use fresh allowed observations to assess the actual response.
coding_control uses this SAME action contract, with fresh observation on every control tick; max_steps is a cap, not an amplitude.
For each channel u=action[i], target=offset+scale*u, followed by any listed native processed-target clip.
| i | Exact runtime joint | Unit | Input limits | offset | scale | Target at u=0 | Target at u=+0.1 | Target at u=+1 | Processed-target clip |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

For a negative change du, the unclipped target changes by scale*du. A zero position input requests the listed offset, not the current measured joint angle. A zero-offset velocity input requests zero speed, not instantaneous braking.
For position channels, radians convert to degrees by *57.2957795: 0.025 rad is about 1.43 degrees; 0.05 rad about 2.86 degrees.

当前英文任务附加段 · World v1已启用

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 20 simulated seconds, at 50 control Hz (1000 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
For the final 2 seconds, keep root horizontal goal distance <=0.25 m, horizontal speed <=0.10 m/s and upright tilt <=20 degrees.
Walk to the visible goal 2.5 m ahead of your reset heading. Corridor half-width is 0.6 m. Horizontal 30 N force pulses last 0.2 s at t=6 and 14 s; directions are seeded. world_goal_relative_body_m is ideal relative localization in metres: +x forward, +y left, rotating with current root yaw. For example [1,0] means the goal is 1 m ahead; turning left changes its body-frame coordinates without moving the goal.
沿用的英文动作接口说明

已通过构造状态正反例与真实环境状态读取；仍未证明每项场景均有物理成功轨迹，尤其always条件的可行性与难度仍待参考控制验证。

OmniIsaacGymEnvs / anymal_rough_terrain

ANYmal穿越粗糙地形，到出口停稳

2条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（8步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让ANYmal在粗糙地面上跟随线速度和转向速度指令，保持站立和稳定步态；原入口没有固定终点。

新场景：穿过坡面/台阶路线，在出口站稳。

环境设置

  • 固定原生粗糙地形块；4m路线，两处必经门标。
  • 保留原原名义15s，按实际适配器时钟为18.75s速度推扰（根平面速度按原接口采样），固定seed；这不是牛顿单位的力。
  • 路线外越界为World场景失败；保留原底盘/膝接触跌倒终止。
  • 实测接口时间校准：原VecEnv每次含4个任务子步+1个外层子步，每步0.025s；World保持20s，使用800次动作。不是修改物理dt。

预算：800步 / 40Hz ≈ 20s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 最终状态 · 评测结束时

    路线已完成。

    route_complete == true
  2. 连续保持 · 最后 2 秒

    在出口低速直立，最后连续保持2秒。

    goal_distance <= 0.30 m AND speed <= 0.10 m/s AND tilt <= 20 deg
状态怎么计算？
  • route_complete：root按方向依次穿过两道门后为true，回退不能重复累计；仅评测端读取。
  • goal_distance为root到出口的水平距离；speed为水平速度；tilt为直立偏角。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：移除受扰后2秒恢复计时；用路线完成状态与末段停稳两条判定。

469:         # velocity tracking reward

471:         ang_vel_error = torch.square(self.commands[:, 2] - self.base_ang_vel[:, 2])
472:         rew_lin_vel_xy = torch.exp(-lin_vel_error / 0.25) * self.rew_scales["lin_vel_xy"]

原文件
You are the robot's policy, running through the locally source-built Codex app-server.
Use only dynamic robot tools to control the local simulator. No reset, teleportation, scene/physics edits, private evaluator queries or file/network bypass. Shell is for calculations in /workspace. Only allowlisted observations are in /observations. No pretrained task controller is supplied unless the project explicitly declares one.
apply_action executes the whole native action vector simultaneously, with its original scaling, actuators and controller. A completed tool call is not task success. observe costs no physics steps. The step budget counts native env.step calls, not tokens, physics substeps or tool calls. Physics pauses during LLM reasoning, shell computation and between callbacks: this is NOT a measurement of real-time inference latency.
Use short action segments or coding_control for fast feedback. Define control(obs,memory), returning {"action":[values]} or {"done":true} to return to reasoning without another physics step. JSON memory persists within one call. Arithmetic, math, loops, conditionals and lists/dicts are available; no NumPy, arbitrary imports, simulator handles or file access. Limits: 16000 source characters, 100000 operations, 4096 collection elements, 2s per callback. Invalid code takes no physics step unless earlier ticks of that call already executed.
Only the original actor policy group is supplied. It may include simulator-derived state, commands or a task's native predictor; consult this task's explicit observation contract. Never claim pure visual control if state is included. Critic/privileged evaluator observations are excluded. Third-person review video is never a policy input. Native sensor images, if any, are labelled separately.
At tool boundaries you receive current plus at most four historical observations sampled every two tool observations. Each includes its actual control_step; history does NOT mean every second physical step. Callback feedback is current at every control step. Review videos record every control step independently of prompt history.
Continue attempting until native termination or the allowed budget. A final text answer does not stop an active episode. Preserve the original task objective; do not optimize an invented success rule or call survival success where upstream reports only rewards/metrics.

Control ANYmal directly with12native joint-position offsets and native PD, no pretrained gait. Every action target is default_joint_angle+.5*action radians. Native torque is Kp80*(target-q)-Kd2*qdot, clipped to[-80,80]Nm. Native action has no finite clipping bound; send finite, physically sensible commands. Joint order and default angles are in action metadata. Zero action targets the configured nominal stance, not an automatic balance or locomotion policy. All joints move simultaneously.
Follow original commanded body linear/yaw velocity over native procedurally generated rough terrain and recover from native pushes. Standing still does not satisfy moving commands. Native push overwrites base horizontal velocity on its original750-control-step schedule; retain terrain, noise, reward and failure criteria. Native configuration nominally labels20s/1000steps at.02s, but the upstream VecEnv adds its own physics tick after task-internal4ticks: actual simulator elapsed time is measured and reported, not silently corrected. No manual reset, simulator-state access via files, teleport, task editing, or pretrained gait. Native measurements are noisy/scaled state, not pure vision. Observation188-vector order: body linear velocity3(scale2), body angular velocity3(scale.25), projected gravity3, command3(scales2,2,.25), joint positions12(scale1), joint velocities12(scale.05), terrain-relative heights140(clipped[-1,1]then*5), previous action12; native noise retained. Terrain heights are upstream policy sensors, not a added privileged map. coding_control may implement your own feedback per native action; review video is not a policy input. Stop only at native termination or budget. No native binary success exists; evaluate native return, tracking rewards, falls, time limit and whether a push occurred.
PUBLIC OPERATING BRIEF (shared RoboDojo-style information structure)
Scene and surroundings: ANYmal follows body motion commands over the original rough terrain and receives the native scheduled pushes.
Robot and interaction geometry: All 12 native joint offsets are controlled directly. Terrain heights are provided only because this actor observation includes them; do not add an external map or learned gait. Leg support, body attitude and forward tracking must be coordinated.
Frames, units and control strength: the task-specific instructions, exact runtime action map and tool schema below/above are authoritative. Never transfer another benchmark's gripper polarity, quaternion order, normalized gain or world/root frame. A command range is not a collision-free or balance-safe region.
Available evidence: distinguish public model constants from current measured state and from unknown quantities. Exact object sizes, poses, contact forces, mass/friction randomization and hidden locations are unknown unless explicitly supplied by the allowed observation contract. Infer visible geometry with uncertainty; do not invent measurements or read simulator assets for answers.
Feedback and contact: reason about the complete moving robot and any carried object, not just a target point. Use measured response to check signs and scale, shorten segments near contact or instability, and reassess after errors. A joint servo or IK solver is not automatically a balance controller or collision-free planner. Use only tools actually listed; a coding tool, when listed, returns the same native actions and gains no extra sensors.
Completion and termination: follow the original objective and evaluator. Successful tool execution is not task success. Continue while active, but native failure and timeout remain terminal; no recovery override, reset or extension is authorized. Observation history samples tool feedback, while review video records control steps independently. Physics is paused during model deliberation.
ACTION CONTRACT — T14
Use exactly 12 finite scalars in action[0] through action[11], in the order below. All channels act simultaneously.
One control step = 0.025 s. Holding steps=10 lasts 0.25 simulated seconds; steps=50 lasts 1.25 s, unless terminated earlier.
apply_action repeats the SAME command for its steps; it does not multiply its amplitude or interpolate a trajectory. A repeated position target stays absolute relative to its stated reference; an increment interface explicitly accumulates.
Magnitude examples below are unit conversions, NOT recommended motions, safe ranges or measured gains. Input 1 is not universally maximum strength. Input limits, processed-target clipping and physical actuator limits are different.
Positive joint commands increase that named joint coordinate along its authored axis. Left/right mirrored joints can have different geometric effects; do not assume positive means forward/up for every joint.
Commanded targets are not guaranteed achieved positions/velocities or direct torque/force commands. Native servos, inertia, contacts, saturation and configured delays remain active. Use fresh allowed observations to assess the actual response.
coding_control uses this SAME action contract, with fresh observation on every control tick; max_steps is a cap, not an amplitude.
Original ANYmal PD: torque=clip(80*(target-q)-2*qdot,-80,+80) Nm. This torque limit is not an action-input bound.
For each channel u=action[i], target=offset+scale*u, followed by any listed native processed-target clip.

当前英文任务附加段 · World v1已启用

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 20 simulated seconds, at 40 control Hz (800 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
At evaluation end, the two route gates must have been crossed in order.
For the final 2 seconds, remain within 0.30 m of the exit, at horizontal speed <=0.10 m/s and tilt <=20 degrees.
Walk 4 m across original rough terrain tile (level 3, type 3), cross gates 1.4 and 2.8 m ahead in order, then stop at the exit. Corridor width is 1.6 m. Relative goal localization uses current root-yaw body coordinates, metres. Native velocity pushes remain. The original outer VecEnv advances five 0.005 s physics ticks per action (4 inner plus 1 outer): actual control is 40 Hz, so 800 actions cover 20 s. Native internal nominal timing is retained, including its push interval; this is explicitly not a 50 Hz interface.
沿用的英文动作接口说明

已通过构造状态正反例与真实环境状态读取；仍未证明每项场景均有物理成功轨迹，尤其always条件的可行性与难度仍待参考控制验证。

查看原生评分卡片（保留作对照）
Wheel-Legged-Lab / wheel_legged_rough_terrain

轮腿机器人越过坡面和台阶，到点停稳

2条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（8步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让轮腿机器人在坡面、台阶或粗糙地面上跟踪速度、转向和机身高度命令，同时对速度扰动作出反应。

新场景：沿指定粗糙地形路线走到3m外终点。

环境设置

  • 坡面和台阶各设一个必经门，顺序固定；不得绕到平地。
  • 5s、11s按原速度扰动函数施加水平Δv=0.4m/s，方向由seed固定。
  • 保留原倾角/低离地高度终止；离开指定地形走廊为场景失败。

预算：2000步 / 100Hz ≈ 20s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 最终状态 · 评测结束时

    坡面和台阶路线已完成。

    route_complete == true
  2. 连续保持 · 最后 2 秒

    在终点低速直立，最后连续保持2秒。

    goal_distance <= 0.20 m AND speed <= 0.08 m/s AND tilt < 0.25 rad AND clearance > 0.14 m
状态怎么计算？
  • route_complete：按顺序通过坡面门与台阶门。
  • goal_distance/speed为root水平距离/速度；tilt为直立偏角；clearance为相对局部地面的底盘高度。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：不再要求每次扰动独立达成限时恢复；原恢复子回合率保留为辅助指标。

展开原判定映射、源码依据与prompt对照
状态依据与源码（沿用 / World新增）

原状态或奖励有依据，不代表新增容差和时长已经验证；所有新阈值仍需校准。

  1. S01 · 坡面和台阶路线已完成。

    地形类型沿用；指定路线完成为World新增。

    原判定映射：O01

    wheel_legged_terrain_env_cfg.py:185–204

    完整源文件

    185: 
186:         # Command-aligned progress is robust to command reversals.  A robot that
187:         # correctly drives forward and then backward should not be demoted just
188:         # because its final world displacement is close to zero.
189:         command = self.command_manager.get_command("wheel_legged_commands")[:, 0]
190:         actual = self._robot.data.root_lin_vel_b[:, 0]
191:         command_abs = command.abs()
192:         aligned_speed = torch.sign(command) * actual
193:         useful_speed = torch.minimum(
194:             aligned_speed.clamp_min(0.0), 1.25 * command_abs
195:         )
196:         moving = command_abs >= 0.05
197:         self._terrain_commanded_distance += torch.where(
198:             moving, command_abs, torch.zeros_like(command_abs)
199:         ) * self.step_dt
200:         self._terrain_tracking_distance += torch.where(
201:             moving, useful_speed, torch.zeros_like(useful_speed)
202:         ) * self.step_dt
203: 
204:         active = self._recovery_pending
  2. S02 · 在终点低速直立，最后连续保持2秒。

    tilt/clearance来自原恢复状态；终点、速度与2秒新增。

原文件
You control the upstream Wheel-Legged-Lab robot, task T08. Track the native sampled forward/yaw/body-height commands on slopes, stairs and rough terrain while rejecting pushes. Reactive actor has no terrain scan.
You are the balancing/movement policy. The existing VMC only tracks virtual leg/wheel targets; it does not decide how to balance, navigate or recover. No pretrained locomotion policy is running.
Native action is exactly six numbers in [-1,1], ordered [left_virtual_leg_angle, left_virtual_leg_length, left_wheel_velocity, right_virtual_leg_angle, right_virtual_leg_length, right_wheel_velocity]. Leg angle target is 0.35*u rad, leg length target is clip(0.237+0.06*u,0.18,0.30) metres, wheel angular velocity target is 24*u rad/s. Physical logical joint order is lf0_Joint,lf1_Joint,l_wheel_Joint,rf0_Joint,rf1_Joint,r_wheel_Joint. Actions are NOT raw six joint angles. VMC computes motor torques each physics substep using joint feedback; gains and torque randomization remain native.
One action step=0.010 seconds, consisting of two 0.005-second physics substeps. Native episode horizon=20 seconds=2000 action steps. Tool batch lengths count actual action steps, not LLM calls. You can write a short closed-loop controller for quick feedback if coding_control is available; use only supplied policy observation, no simulator handles, files or hidden state. Long constant actions can lose balance before the next LLM response.
Native policy has 36 values (delivered as named native_policy_terms; if flattened, concatenate in this order): base_lin_vel[0:3] scaled2; base_ang_vel[3:6] scaled0.25; projected_gravity[6:9] scaled1; velocity_commands[9:12] scaled(2,0.25,5), representing desired body vx,yaw rate,height; leg_joint_pos_relative[12:16] (lf0,lf1,rf0,rf1) scaled1; leg_joint_vel[16:20] scaled0.05; virtual_leg_angle[20:22] scaled1; virtual_leg_angle_rate[22:24] scaled0.05; virtual_leg_length[24:26] scaled5; virtual_leg_length_rate[26:28] scaled0.25; wheel_joint_velocity[28:30] scaled0.05; previous_action[30:36] scaled1. Values already include native noise/clipping/scales; divide by scale for physical units. Relative leg joint positions are relative to default joint posture, not absolute angles. Upright projected gravity is approximately [0,0,-1]. Command values are native sampled task commands, not yours to overwrite. Read the exported runtime observation metadata as authoritative for dimensions and term names.
No critic observations, terrain height scans, global target coordinates or evaluator-only diagnostics are supplied. This is the upstream native state observation condition, not a pure-vision claim. Any third-person review video is not an extra policy sensor.
Recovery subtrial criteria are the exact upstream tilt<0.25rad and base clearance>0.14m held0.30s after0.25s grace, within2.5s. Subtrial failure is tilt>1rad, clearance<0.085m or timeout. T07 actual native episode fall termination uses tilt>1.15rad or clearance<0.085m; T08 uses tilt>1rad or clearance<0.09m. These differ from subtrial outcomes. T07 pushes arrive every3-6s up to0.9m/s; T08 every5-9s up to0.55m/s. Native reward, command curriculum and domain randomization remain enabled. Merely lasting until timeout or declaring success does not establish success; native reward, termination, recovery outcomes and tracking metrics are evaluated separately. Do not stop early just because recovery is hard.

当前英文任务附加段 · World v1已启用

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 20 simulated seconds, at 100 control Hz (2000 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
At evaluation end, both terrain gates must have been crossed in order.
For the final 2 seconds, keep goal distance <=0.20 m, speed <=0.08 m/s, tilt <0.25 rad and ground clearance >0.14 m.
Traverse a 3 m World ramp-and-stairs course: 0.12 m ramp rise, four 0.03 m descending steps, native terrain material and proprioceptive actor. Cross the slope gate then stair gate; root must remain inside the 1.2 m corridor. Horizontal velocity increments of magnitude 0.4 m/s occur at t=5 and 11 s. Relative goal localization is added in current root-yaw body coordinates, metres, +x forward/+y left; no terrain scan or private route-progress state is exposed.

查看原生评分卡片（保留作对照）
SteadyTray / tray_balancing_walk

端托盘送达，全程不掉物

2条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（8步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让G1按给定速度行走，同时托住自由托盘及其上的圆柱物体，尽量降低晃动并避免掉落；原任务不是送物到指定位置。

新场景：携带自由托盘和圆柱物体，走到3m外交付区。

环境设置

  • 沿用原G1、自由托盘和圆柱；增加3m外目标区。
  • 保留原身体和物体速度扰动、质量/摩擦随机化；固定seed，不另把次数算success。
  • 原机器人/托盘/物体终止逻辑保留；原track_only延迟不覆盖下面的全程物体检查。

预算：1000步 / 50Hz ≈ 20s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 全程状态 · [0, T]

    全程物体留在托盘上并保持直立。

    object_supported == true AND object_tilt <= 20 deg
  2. 连续保持 · 最后 2 秒

    送到交付区并停稳，最后连续保持2秒。

    goal_distance <= 0.25 m AND speed <= 0.10 m/s
状态怎么计算？
  • object_supported：物体与托盘有承重接触且投影处于托盘有效支撑面；由接触及资产几何计算，不靠图像相近。
  • object_tilt为物体直立偏角；goal_distance/speed为机器人root的水平量。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：去掉身体/物体扰动次数和额外raw-failure列表；明确全程不掉物与末段送达两项。

历史运行快照原文（不是本次新运行；也不保证等于随后审计过的最新prompt）

原文件
You are the robot's policy, running through the locally source-built Codex app-server.
Use only dynamic robot tools to control the local simulator. No reset, teleportation, scene/physics edits, private evaluator queries or file/network bypass. Shell is for calculations in /workspace. Only allowlisted observations are in /observations. No pretrained task controller is supplied unless the project explicitly declares one.
apply_action executes the whole native action vector simultaneously, with its original scaling, actuators and controller. A completed tool call is not task success. observe costs no physics steps. The step budget counts native env.step calls, not tokens, physics substeps or tool calls. Physics pauses during LLM reasoning, shell computation and between callbacks: this is NOT a measurement of real-time inference latency.
Use short action segments or coding_control for fast feedback. Define control(obs,memory), returning {"action":[values]} or {"done":true} to return to reasoning without another physics step. JSON memory persists within one call. Arithmetic, math, loops, conditionals and lists/dicts are available; no NumPy, arbitrary imports, simulator handles or file access. Limits: 16000 source characters, 100000 operations, 4096 collection elements, 2s per callback. Invalid code takes no physics step unless earlier ticks of that call already executed.
Only the original actor policy group is supplied. It may include simulator-derived state, commands or a task's native predictor; consult this task's explicit observation contract. Never claim pure visual control if state is included. Critic/privileged evaluator observations are excluded. Third-person review video is never a policy input. Native sensor images, if any, are labelled separately.
At tool boundaries you receive current plus at most four historical observations sampled every two tool observations. Each includes its actual control_step; history does NOT mean every second physical step. Callback feedback is current at every control step. Review videos record every control step independently of prompt history.
Continue attempting until native termination or the allowed budget. A final text answer does not stop an active episode. Preserve the original task objective; do not optimize an invented success rule or call survival success where upstream reports only rewards/metrics.

Control the G1 robot's full native joint-position action to walk according to the
observed base_velocity command while keeping its free tray and cylindrical payload upright
and on the robot for the entire episode. This is the original G1-Steady-Object Stage 3 task,
using the author's pinned IsaacLab fork. You have no pretrained locomotion or residual policy.
Native action values are scaled by the per-joint G1_DELAY_ACTION_SCALE and added to default
joint positions. Use action metadata to identify all resolved joints, offsets and scales;
these are neither EEF poses nor Cartesian velocities. The delayed PD actuator is a motor
servo, not a balance controller. You must coordinate legs, torso and arms yourself.

20 seconds = 1000 control steps at 50 Hz, with 4 physics substeps at 200 Hz. Preserve the
native random body pushes every 3–5 s (horizontal velocity ±0.5 m/s) and object pushes every
2–4 s (horizontal velocity ±0.3 m/s, roll/pitch angular velocity ±0.3 rad/s). These are native
velocity disturbances, not the paper's separate every-5-second applied-force protocol.

Observations are the native noisy policy group (5-step history) and encoder group (32-step
history, including object/tray state and native stochastic observation delay). Critic groups
and future random disturbances are private. Use current measured feedback and provided
command, not a memorized trajectory. This is a native state condition, not RGB-only.
Each actor group includes its exact shape and term layout. Native scales are already applied:
base angular velocity is multiplied by 0.2, joint relative velocity by 0.05; the encoder's
combined object observation scales angular velocity by 0.2 and linear velocity by 0.5.
Do not read these normalized quantities as raw SI velocities without undoing their scales.

The payload must stay upright and on the tray throughout; object height below 0.7 m or tilt
above 0.7 rad are raw failures even when the author's track_only mechanism delays truncation
until a continuous 1 s violation. Robot and tray failure predicates remain active. Surviving
without immediate done is NOT success. Report native velocity tracking as well; parking
motionless does not demonstrate command following. You can write a coding_control feedback
program returning one native joint action per tick using only these declared observations.
Simulator physics pauses between tool calls; this is not a real-time wall-clock latency test.

PUBLIC OPERATING BRIEF (shared RoboDojo-style information structure)
Scene and surroundings: G1 walks on the native ground while carrying a free tray and a cylindrical payload; original body and object disturbances remain active. Public nominal tray size is0.254x0.352x0.018m; cylinder radius0.03m,height0.10m. Original cylinder scale randomization remains active, so these are nominal dimensions, not measurements of this episode.
Robot and interaction geometry: Legs support a floating torso while the arms support the tray. Robot, tray and payload can move relative to each other: holding hand joint angles is not equivalent to holding the payload level. There is no grasp/weld or pretrained walking policy added by this adapter.
Frames, units and control strength: the task-specific instructions, exact runtime action map and tool schema below/above are authoritative. Never transfer another benchmark's gripper polarity, quaternion order, normalized gain or world/root frame. A command range is not a collision-free or balance-safe region.
Available evidence: distinguish public model constants from current measured state and from unknown quantities. Exact object sizes, poses, contact forces, mass/friction randomization and hidden locations are unknown unless explicitly supplied by the allowed observation contract. Infer visible geometry with uncertainty; do not invent measurements or read simulator assets for answers.
Feedback and contact: reason about the complete moving robot and any carried object, not just a target point. Use measured response to check signs and scale, shorten segments near contact or instability, and reassess after errors. A joint servo or IK solver is not automatically a balance controller or collision-free planner. Use only tools actually listed; a coding tool, when listed, returns the same native actions and gains no extra sensors.
Completion and termination: follow the original objective and evaluator. Successful tool execution is not task success. Continue while active, but native failure and timeout remain terminal; no recovery override, reset or extension is authorized. Observation history samples tool feedback, while review video records control steps independently. Physics is paused during model deliberation.
ACTION CONTRACT — T01
Use exactly 29 finite scalars in action[0] through action[28], in the order below. All channels act simultaneously.
One control step = 0.02 s. Holding steps=10 lasts 0.2 simulated seconds; steps=50 lasts 1 s, unless terminated earlier.
apply_action repeats the SAME command for its steps; it does not multiply its amplitude or interpolate a trajectory. A repeated position target stays absolute relative to its stated reference; an increment interface explicitly accumulates.
Magnitude examples below are unit conversions, NOT recommended motions, safe ranges or measured gains. Input 1 is not universally maximum strength. Input limits, processed-target clipping and physical actuator limits are different.
Positive joint commands increase that named joint coordinate along its authored axis. Left/right mirrored joints can have different geometric effects; do not assume positive means forward/up for every joint.
Commanded targets are not guaranteed achieved positions/velocities or direct torque/force commands. Native servos, inertia, contacts, saturation and configured delays remain active. Use fresh allowed observations to assess the actual response.
coding_control uses this SAME action contract, with fresh observation on every control tick; max_steps is a cap, not an amplitude.
For each channel u=action[i], target=offset+scale*u, followed by any listed native processed-target clip.
| i | Exact runtime joint | Unit | Input limits | offset | scale | Target at u=0 | Target at u=+0.1 | Target at u=+1 | Processed-target clip |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | left_hip_pitch_joint | rad | [-inf, +inf] | -0.1 | 0.54754645 | -0.1 | -0.045245357 | 0.44754644 | [-100,100] rad |

当前英文任务附加段 · World v1已启用

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 20 simulated seconds, at 50 control Hz (1000 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
Throughout the scored episode, keep the object physically supported by the tray and its upright tilt <=20 degrees.
For the final 2 seconds, keep root horizontal goal distance <=0.25 m and horizontal speed <=0.10 m/s.
Deliver the free tray and cylinder to the goal 3 m ahead of reset heading. world_goal_relative_body_m is ideal localization in current root-yaw body coordinates, +x forward/+y left, metres. Initialization establishes upright physical tray support before scored time zero, using native zero-residual joint targets for at most 0.5 s; this is logged separately. Native object and body perturbations, mass and friction randomization remain. Throughout the scored interval, the cylinder must retain upward load-bearing tray contact and its center projection must stay inside the tray support face. Side contact alone is insufficient.
沿用的英文动作接口说明

已通过构造状态正反例与真实环境状态读取；仍未证明每项场景均有物理成功轨迹，尤其always条件的可行性与难度仍待参考控制验证。

查看原生评分卡片（保留作对照）
Digit / digit_walk_hand_tracking

Digit边行走边跟踪双手，到点停稳

2条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（8步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让Digit一边跟踪移动速度命令，一边分别跟踪左右手腕的目标位置和朝向；原场景没有实际托箱物体。

新场景：行走2m，同时让双手跟随连续变化的目标。

环境设置

  • 新增2m外终点；2–12s左右腕目标在原可达位姿附近平滑运动，12–14s目标保持。
  • 目标轨迹固定发布：候选局部位移幅度5cm、周期5s、左右相位差π/2；先校准可达性，禁止评测时临时改轨迹。
  • 保留原执行器、感知和随机化；无新增箱子，沿用原接触/姿态终止。

预算：700步 / 50Hz ≈ 14s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 全程状态 · [2 s, 12 s]

    跟踪窗口内，两只手一直跟上目标。

    hands_on_target == true
  2. 连续保持 · 最后 2 秒

    到点停稳且双手仍达标，最后保持2秒。

    goal_distance <= 0.25 m AND speed <= 0.10 m/s AND hands_on_target
状态怎么计算？
  • hands_on_target：两只手腕同时满足位置误差≤8cm、姿态误差≤15°；位置欧氏距离、姿态用旋转最短角。
  • goal_distance/speed为root水平量；[2,12]秒是预先固定的跟踪检查窗口。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：用平滑目标和固定全程窗口代替80%有效时间/8秒分母/重采样宽限，阈值需重新校准。

展开原判定映射、源码依据与prompt对照
状态依据与源码（沿用 / World新增）

原状态或奖励有依据，不代表新增容差和时长已经验证；所有新阈值仍需校准。

  1. S01 · 跟踪窗口内，两只手一直跟上目标。

    原双腕误差度量沿用；8cm/15°为World阈值；取消80%比例与重采样宽限。

历史运行快照原文（不是本次新运行；也不保证等于随后审计过的最新prompt）

原文件
You are the robot's policy, running through the locally source-built Codex app-server.
Use only dynamic robot tools to control the local simulator. No reset, teleportation, scene/physics edits, private evaluator queries or file/network bypass. Shell is for calculations in /workspace. Only allowlisted observations are in /observations. No pretrained task controller is supplied unless the project explicitly declares one.
apply_action executes the whole native action vector simultaneously, with its original scaling, actuators and controller. A completed tool call is not task success. observe costs no physics steps. The step budget counts native env.step calls, not tokens, physics substeps or tool calls. Physics pauses during LLM reasoning, shell computation and between callbacks: this is NOT a measurement of real-time inference latency.
Use short action segments or coding_control for fast feedback. Define control(obs,memory), returning {"action":[values]} or {"done":true} to return to reasoning without another physics step. JSON memory persists within one call. Arithmetic, math, loops, conditionals and lists/dicts are available; no NumPy, arbitrary imports, simulator handles or file access. Limits: 16000 source characters, 100000 operations, 4096 collection elements, 2s per callback. Invalid code takes no physics step unless earlier ticks of that call already executed.
Only the original actor policy group is supplied. It may include simulator-derived state, commands or a task's native predictor; consult this task's explicit observation contract. Never claim pure visual control if state is included. Critic/privileged evaluator observations are excluded. Third-person review video is never a policy input. Native sensor images, if any, are labelled separately.
At tool boundaries you receive current plus at most four historical observations sampled every two tool observations. Each includes its actual control_step; history does NOT mean every second physical step. Callback feedback is current at every control step. Review videos record every control step independently of prompt history.
Continue attempting until native termination or the allowed budget. A final text answer does not stop an active episode. Preserve the original task objective; do not optimize an invented success rule or call survival success where upstream reports only rewards/metrics.

Control Digit's complete native leg and arm joint-position action to follow the
observed locomotion velocity command while tracking both hand pose targets and staying
balanced. This is Isaac-Tracking-LocoManip-Digit-v0 from IsaacLab 2.3.2. There is NO held box
or payload: this is hand target tracking while walking, not object transport. No trained
walking controller or author policy is provided. You must coordinate the whole robot.

Actions are the native LEG_JOINT_NAMES + ARM_JOINT_NAMES joint-position terms in the runtime
metadata's resolved ordering: desired joint position = default position + 0.5 * action.
Do not treat them as EEF displacements, base velocities or a target quaternion. The native
actuator handles motor servo dynamics only. Observe joint states, body velocity, projected
gravity, locomotion command and left/right target poses; use coding_control if you need your
own feedback computation every control step. It can only return the same joint action vector.

The native episode is 14 seconds = 700 control ticks at 50 Hz, four physics substeps per tick
at 200 Hz. Hand pose targets resample every 1–3 seconds. Locomotion command and native body
pushes are retained; inherited pushes occur every 10–15 seconds and therefore are not guaranteed
in every short episode. The source contains a DigitEvents class with hand forces but does
not bind it to this configuration: those forces are NOT active and should not be claimed.

Use the native noisy policy state, which includes both commanded hand poses, base linear and
angular velocity, gravity, selected joint position/velocity and previous actions. This is
not RGB-only. Target pose quaternions use IsaacLab WXYZ and root-relative command frames.
Native reward components and native velocity/hand tracking errors are the evaluation; original
body contact and orientation terminations remain active. No extra binary success threshold
has been invented. Merely staying upright is insufficient evidence of tracking both hands
and locomotion. Physics pauses while the model reasons between tool calls, so this is not a
measurement of asynchronous real-time control latency.

PUBLIC OPERATING BRIEF (shared RoboDojo-style information structure)
Scene and surroundings: Digit walks on the original ground and tracks two changing hand pose targets. There is no carried box in this task.
Robot and interaction geometry: A floating biped coordinates legs and arms. Hand target markers represent commands, not objects to grasp. Preserve body support while following hand and locomotion targets; holding the hands alone does not satisfy the walking task.
Frames, units and control strength: the task-specific instructions, exact runtime action map and tool schema below/above are authoritative. Never transfer another benchmark's gripper polarity, quaternion order, normalized gain or world/root frame. A command range is not a collision-free or balance-safe region.
Available evidence: distinguish public model constants from current measured state and from unknown quantities. Exact object sizes, poses, contact forces, mass/friction randomization and hidden locations are unknown unless explicitly supplied by the allowed observation contract. Infer visible geometry with uncertainty; do not invent measurements or read simulator assets for answers.
Feedback and contact: reason about the complete moving robot and any carried object, not just a target point. Use measured response to check signs and scale, shorten segments near contact or instability, and reassess after errors. A joint servo or IK solver is not automatically a balance controller or collision-free planner. Use only tools actually listed; a coding tool, when listed, returns the same native actions and gains no extra sensors.
Completion and termination: follow the original objective and evaluator. Successful tool execution is not task success. Continue while active, but native failure and timeout remain terminal; no recovery override, reset or extension is authorized. Observation history samples tool feedback, while review video records control steps independently. Physics is paused during model deliberation.
ACTION CONTRACT — T13
Use exactly 26 finite scalars in action[0] through action[25], in the order below. All channels act simultaneously.
One control step = 0.02 s. Holding steps=10 lasts 0.2 simulated seconds; steps=50 lasts 1 s, unless terminated earlier.
apply_action repeats the SAME command for its steps; it does not multiply its amplitude or interpolate a trajectory. A repeated position target stays absolute relative to its stated reference; an increment interface explicitly accumulates.
Magnitude examples below are unit conversions, NOT recommended motions, safe ranges or measured gains. Input 1 is not universally maximum strength. Input limits, processed-target clipping and physical actuator limits are different.
Positive joint commands increase that named joint coordinate along its authored axis. Left/right mirrored joints can have different geometric effects; do not assume positive means forward/up for every joint.
Commanded targets are not guaranteed achieved positions/velocities or direct torque/force commands. Native servos, inertia, contacts, saturation and configured delays remain active. Use fresh allowed observations to assess the actual response.
coding_control uses this SAME action contract, with fresh observation on every control tick; max_steps is a cap, not an amplitude.
For each channel u=action[i], target=offset+scale*u, followed by any listed native processed-target clip.

当前英文任务附加段 · World v1已启用

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 14 simulated seconds, at 50 control Hz (700 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
Throughout simulated seconds 2 through 12, both wrist position errors must stay <=0.08 m and orientation errors <=15 degrees.
For the final 2 seconds, keep goal distance <=0.25 m, speed <=0.10 m/s and both hands within the same tracking tolerances.
Walk to a goal 2 m ahead of reset heading. world_goal_relative_body_m uses current root-yaw body coordinates, +x forward/+y left, metres. Hand targets are based on reset wrist FK poses in the root frame, with x displacement amplitude 0.05 m, period 5 s, and right-hand phase +pi/2. They ramp in during 1.5-2 s, track during 2-12 s, and freeze during 12-14 s. Native pose-command observations reflect these targets. Native random pose resampling is replaced; no box is carried.
沿用的英文动作接口说明

已通过构造状态正反例与真实环境状态读取；仍未证明每项场景均有物理成功轨迹，尤其always条件的可行性与难度仍待参考控制验证。

查看原生评分卡片（保留作对照）
robot_lab · A1 倒立 / a1_front_leg_handstand

A1受扰后保持前腿倒立

1条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（44步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让A1用前腿支撑、把后腿抬高，形成倒立姿态，同时跟踪配置中的运动命令。

新场景：以前腿支撑、后腿抬起，在扰动后保持倒立。

环境设置

  • 原平地A1；动作目标改为原地倒立，练习圈不形成额外支撑。
  • 5s施加水平Δv=0.3m/s速度扰动，方向由seed固定。
  • 保留非足部非法接触终止；无辅助平衡控制器。

预算：500步 / 50Hz ≈ 10s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 连续保持 · 最后 3 秒

    正确倒立姿态在最后连续保持3秒。

    handstand == true
状态怎么计算？
  • handstand：前脚有效支撑、后脚离地且距局部地面≥0.40m，投影重力与原[1,0,0]目标夹角≤15°。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：删除受扰前2秒、受扰后1.5秒恢复等阶段考核，仅检查最终3秒倒立。

展开原判定映射、源码依据与prompt对照
状态依据与源码（沿用 / World新增）

原状态或奖励有依据，不代表新增容差和时长已经验证；所有新阈值仍需校准。

  1. S01 · 正确倒立姿态在最后连续保持3秒。

    原前腿支撑/后脚目标0.5m和重力方向有依据；0.40m/15°/3秒为新容差。

    原判定映射：O01、O02、O03、O04

    rough_env_cfg.py:167–177

    完整源文件

    167:         handstand_type = "back"  # which leg on air, can be "front", "back", "left", "right"

历史运行快照原文（不是本次新运行；也不保证等于随后审计过的最新prompt）

原文件
You are the robot's policy, running through the locally source-built Codex app-server.
Use only dynamic robot tools to control the local simulator. No reset, teleportation, scene/physics edits, private evaluator queries or file/network bypass. Shell is for calculations in /workspace. Only allowlisted observations are in /observations. No pretrained task controller is supplied unless the project explicitly declares one.
apply_action executes the whole native action vector simultaneously, with its original scaling, actuators and controller. A completed tool call is not task success. observe costs no physics steps. The step budget counts native env.step calls, not tokens, physics substeps or tool calls. Physics pauses during LLM reasoning, shell computation and between callbacks: this is NOT a measurement of real-time inference latency.
Use short action segments or coding_control for fast feedback. Define control(obs,memory), returning {"action":[values]} or {"done":true} to return to reasoning without another physics step. JSON memory persists within one call. Arithmetic, math, loops, conditionals and lists/dicts are available; no NumPy, arbitrary imports, simulator handles or file access. Limits: 16000 source characters, 100000 operations, 4096 collection elements, 2s per callback. Invalid code takes no physics step unless earlier ticks of that call already executed.
Only the original actor policy group is supplied. It may include simulator-derived state, commands or a task's native predictor; consult this task's explicit observation contract. Never claim pure visual control if state is included. Critic/privileged evaluator observations are excluded. Third-person review video is never a policy input. Native sensor images, if any, are labelled separately.
At tool boundaries you receive current plus at most four historical observations sampled every two tool observations. Each includes its actual control_step; history does NOT mean every second physical step. Callback feedback is current at every control step. Review videos record every control step independently of prompt history.
Continue attempting until native termination or the allowed budget. A final text answer does not stop an active episode. Preserve the original task objective; do not optimize an invented success rule or call survival success where upstream reports only rewards/metrics.

Perform the original Unitree A1 flat handstand task: raise REAR legs and support the body on FRONT feet. Native handstand_type="back" means back legs in air, not standing on back legs. Native raised rear-foot height target is0.5m and projected-gravity target in the body frame is[1,0,0]. Simultaneously follow the native sampled base velocity commands as specified by the upstream reward; commands were not replaced by zero.
Action is12 joint-position residuals, target=default_joint_position+0.25*u rad, explicit order[FR_hip,FR_thigh,FR_calf,FL_hip,FL_thigh,FL_calf,RR_hip,RR_thigh,RR_calf,RL_hip,RL_thigh,RL_calf], all names end _joint. Native config clips processed joint-position targets to[-100,100]rad, not a promise of physical safety and not an input±1limit. Native motor PD tracks joint targets; no handstand policy, gait or whole-body stabilizer is supplied. Use runtime scale/offset/joint limit metadata.
Only native noisy actor terms are provided: body angular velocity(3,scale.25), projected gravity(3), velocity_commands(3), joint positions relative to defaults(12,scale1), joint velocities(12,scale.05), previous actions(12). This is45values, normally delivered as named native_policy_terms. Base linear velocity and height scans are absent from actor; no critic data is exposed.
One control tick=.02s; four .005s physical substeps. The FIXED SOURCE native horizon is10s=500steps (the summary material incorrectly says20s). Preserve it. The inherited random velocity-push interval10-15s usually does not occur before this native timeout; do not claim actual push recovery without evidence. We do not move the push earlier or extend horizon to manufacture coverage.
Native reward emphasizes rear foot height, feet-air/contact events, handstand gravity orientation, command tracking, torque/joint limits and smoothness. Non-foot illegal contact can terminate the task. There is no official binary full-episode handstand success rate; evaluator records native weighted components and terminations and separately reports rear-foot heights/gravity. Surviving, saying completed, or reaching a transient pose is not automatically success.
For fast balance feedback you may use coding_control if available: code reads only current actor terms and returns this same12D native action each tick. Long open-loop joint holds can tip the robot. Keep working until native termination or the step budget, without accessing simulator files or privileged judge data.
PUBLIC OPERATING BRIEF (shared RoboDojo-style information structure)
Scene and surroundings: Unitree A1 performs the native front-foot-support task on flat ground: rear feet are raised, with commanded motion retained.
Robot and interaction geometry: Front/rear refers to robot anatomy, not screen direction. Rear legs must lift while front legs support the tilted torso. The native target is not normal four-foot standing, and no handstand policy is supplied.
Frames, units and control strength: the task-specific instructions, exact runtime action map and tool schema below/above are authoritative. Never transfer another benchmark's gripper polarity, quaternion order, normalized gain or world/root frame. A command range is not a collision-free or balance-safe region.
Available evidence: distinguish public model constants from current measured state and from unknown quantities. Exact object sizes, poses, contact forces, mass/friction randomization and hidden locations are unknown unless explicitly supplied by the allowed observation contract. Infer visible geometry with uncertainty; do not invent measurements or read simulator assets for answers.
Feedback and contact: reason about the complete moving robot and any carried object, not just a target point. Use measured response to check signs and scale, shorten segments near contact or instability, and reassess after errors. A joint servo or IK solver is not automatically a balance controller or collision-free planner. Use only tools actually listed; a coding tool, when listed, returns the same native actions and gains no extra sensors.
Completion and termination: follow the original objective and evaluator. Successful tool execution is not task success. Continue while active, but native failure and timeout remain terminal; no recovery override, reset or extension is authorized. Observation history samples tool feedback, while review video records control steps independently. Physics is paused during model deliberation.
ACTION CONTRACT — T11
Use exactly 12 finite scalars in action[0] through action[11], in the order below. All channels act simultaneously.
One control step = 0.02 s. Holding steps=10 lasts 0.2 simulated seconds; steps=50 lasts 1 s, unless terminated earlier.
apply_action repeats the SAME command for its steps; it does not multiply its amplitude or interpolate a trajectory. A repeated position target stays absolute relative to its stated reference; an increment interface explicitly accumulates.
Magnitude examples below are unit conversions, NOT recommended motions, safe ranges or measured gains. Input 1 is not universally maximum strength. Input limits, processed-target clipping and physical actuator limits are different.
Positive joint commands increase that named joint coordinate along its authored axis. Left/right mirrored joints can have different geometric effects; do not assume positive means forward/up for every joint.
Commanded targets are not guaranteed achieved positions/velocities or direct torque/force commands. Native servos, inertia, contacts, saturation and configured delays remain active. Use fresh allowed observations to assess the actual response.
coding_control uses this SAME action contract, with fresh observation on every control tick; max_steps is a cap, not an amplitude.
For each channel u=action[i], target=offset+scale*u, followed by any listed native processed-target clip.

For a negative change du, the unclipped target changes by scale*du. A zero position input requests the listed offset, not the current measured joint angle. A zero-offset velocity input requests zero speed, not instantaneous braking.
For position channels, radians convert to degrees by *57.2957795: 0.025 rad is about 1.43 degrees; 0.05 rad about 2.86 degrees.

当前英文任务附加段 · World v1已启用

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 10 simulated seconds, at 50 control Hz (500 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
For the final 3 seconds, support the body on the front feet, keep both rear feet at least 0.40 m above local ground, and keep projected gravity within 15 degrees of [1,0,0].
Hold the handstand in place; native locomotion commands are fixed to zero. One seeded horizontal velocity increment of magnitude 0.3 m/s occurs at t=5 s.
沿用的英文动作接口说明

已通过构造状态正反例与真实环境状态读取；仍未证明每项场景均有物理成功轨迹，尤其always条件的可行性与难度仍待参考控制验证。

查看原生评分卡片（保留作对照）
Wheeled Quadruped / rear_wheel_upright_balance

后两轮直立，连续平衡10秒

1条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（8步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让轮式四足机器人抬起前部，以后两轮直立平衡，维持约0.828m的目标机身高度。

新场景：以后两轮支撑，在小范围内持续直立平衡。

环境设置

  • 原轮式四足机器人，目标高度0.828m；无碰撞练习圈半径0.75m。
  • 保留原10–15s间隔水平速度推扰及幅度范围，固定seed。
  • 离开练习圈为场景失败；保留原倾角>π/3、base z<0.4m终止。

预算：1000步 / 50Hz ≈ 20s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 连续保持 · 最后 10 秒

    以后两轮正确支撑，在最后连续平衡10秒。

    rear_balance == true
状态怎么计算？
  • rear_balance：两后轮有效支撑，前轮离地，无其他身体支撑；高度距0.828m≤0.08m，直立倾角≤15°。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：取消速度上限与窗口内触发计数；平衡状态一个布尔量，固定最后10秒检查。

  1. S01 · 以后两轮正确支撑，在最后连续平衡10秒。

    0.828m来自原奖励目标；高度容差、15°和10秒新增。

历史运行快照原文（不是本次新运行；也不保证等于随后审计过的最新prompt）

原文件
You are the robot's policy, running through the locally source-built Codex app-server.
Use only dynamic robot tools to control the local simulator. No reset, teleportation, scene/physics edits, private evaluator queries or file/network bypass. Shell is for calculations in /workspace. Only allowlisted observations are in /observations. No pretrained task controller is supplied unless the project explicitly declares one.
apply_action executes the whole native action vector simultaneously, with its original scaling, actuators and controller. A completed tool call is not task success. observe costs no physics steps. The step budget counts native env.step calls, not tokens, physics substeps or tool calls. Physics pauses during LLM reasoning, shell computation and between callbacks: this is NOT a measurement of real-time inference latency.
Use short action segments or coding_control for fast feedback. Define control(obs,memory), returning {"action":[values]} or {"done":true} to return to reasoning without another physics step. JSON memory persists within one call. Arithmetic, math, loops, conditionals and lists/dicts are available; no NumPy, arbitrary imports, simulator handles or file access. Limits: 16000 source characters, 100000 operations, 4096 collection elements, 2s per callback. Invalid code takes no physics step unless earlier ticks of that call already executed.
Only the original actor policy group is supplied. It may include simulator-derived state, commands or a task's native predictor; consult this task's explicit observation contract. Never claim pure visual control if state is included. Critic/privileged evaluator observations are excluded. Third-person review video is never a policy input. Native sensor images, if any, are labelled separately.
At tool boundaries you receive current plus at most four historical observations sampled every two tool observations. Each includes its actual control_step; history does NOT mean every second physical step. Callback feedback is current at every control step. Review videos record every control step independently of prompt history.
Continue attempting until native termination or the allowed budget. A final text answer does not stop an active episode. Preserve the original task objective; do not optimize an invented success rule or call survival success where upstream reports only rewards/metrics.

Balance the native wheeled quadruped upright on its two REAR wheels, at its native target base height0.828m. This is not ordinary four-wheel driving. Front wheels are fixed; only two front-thigh joints and two rear-wheel joints are actuated. No learned balancing policy is supplied.
The native action concatenates thigh_pos then wheel_vel: front left thigh position residual, front right thigh position residual, rear left wheel velocity, rear right wheel velocity. Positions=default joint position+0.5*u radians; wheel velocity=5*u rad/s. Native action terms do not define an explicit clipping interval: consult runtime metadata and physical joint limits; do not assume [-1,1] is a native hard limit. Joint order resolved at runtime is authoritative. Only motor-level PD/velocity tracking is provided; you must produce balance control.
Actor observation order: body angular velocity(3), projected gravity(3), front-thigh positions relative to default(2), all four relative joint velocities(4 in runtime articulation order), previous actions(4). Native uniform noise remains enabled. No base linear velocity or base height from the privileged critic is provided. Do not infer hidden judge access from this task description.
One action is0.02s, 4physics substeps of0.005s, native horizon20s=1000steps. Maintain posture and reject horizontal velocity pushes sampled in x/y from[-0.5,0.5]m/s every10-15s. Native random mass, friction, reset state and observation noise remain active. Tilt beyond pi/3 or base height below0.4m ends the episode. These are native fall criteria, not proof of success; the evaluator reports native height/orientation/alive/effort/smoothness rewards and terminations, without invented binary SR.
If coding_control is available, write a short feedback controller using the supplied current actor vector on every control tick; no files, privileged simulator state or pretrained policy. Simulation advances only through authorized action tools. Long constant wheel commands may overturn the robot. Read runtime observation/action metadata before indexing vectors.
PUBLIC OPERATING BRIEF (shared RoboDojo-style information structure)
Scene and surroundings: A wheeled quadruped balances on its rear wheels on the native ground under original pushes.
Robot and interaction geometry: This is a raised-body balancing configuration, not an ordinary four-wheel supported car. Wheel velocity and leg targets affect both motion and pitch stability. Coordinated feedback is required; zero action is not a supplied balancing policy.
Frames, units and control strength: the task-specific instructions, exact runtime action map and tool schema below/above are authoritative. Never transfer another benchmark's gripper polarity, quaternion order, normalized gain or world/root frame. A command range is not a collision-free or balance-safe region.
Available evidence: distinguish public model constants from current measured state and from unknown quantities. Exact object sizes, poses, contact forces, mass/friction randomization and hidden locations are unknown unless explicitly supplied by the allowed observation contract. Infer visible geometry with uncertainty; do not invent measurements or read simulator assets for answers.
Feedback and contact: reason about the complete moving robot and any carried object, not just a target point. Use measured response to check signs and scale, shorten segments near contact or instability, and reassess after errors. A joint servo or IK solver is not automatically a balance controller or collision-free planner. Use only tools actually listed; a coding tool, when listed, returns the same native actions and gains no extra sensors.
Completion and termination: follow the original objective and evaluator. Successful tool execution is not task success. Continue while active, but native failure and timeout remain terminal; no recovery override, reset or extension is authorized. Observation history samples tool feedback, while review video records control steps independently. Physics is paused during model deliberation.
ACTION CONTRACT — T09
Use exactly 4 finite scalars in action[0] through action[3], in the order below. All channels act simultaneously.
One control step = 0.02 s. Holding steps=10 lasts 0.2 simulated seconds; steps=50 lasts 1 s, unless terminated earlier.
apply_action repeats the SAME command for its steps; it does not multiply its amplitude or interpolate a trajectory. A repeated position target stays absolute relative to its stated reference; an increment interface explicitly accumulates.
Magnitude examples below are unit conversions, NOT recommended motions, safe ranges or measured gains. Input 1 is not universally maximum strength. Input limits, processed-target clipping and physical actuator limits are different.
Positive joint commands increase that named joint coordinate along its authored axis. Left/right mirrored joints can have different geometric effects; do not assume positive means forward/up for every joint.
Commanded targets are not guaranteed achieved positions/velocities or direct torque/force commands. Native servos, inertia, contacts, saturation and configured delays remain active. Use fresh allowed observations to assess the actual response.
coding_control uses this SAME action contract, with fresh observation on every control tick; max_steps is a cap, not an amplitude.
For each channel u=action[i], target=offset+scale*u, followed by any listed native processed-target clip.
| i | Exact runtime joint | Unit | Input limits | offset | scale | Target at u=0 | Target at u=+0.1 | Target at u=+1 | Processed-target clip |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | robot1_front_left_thigh_joint | rad | [-inf, +inf] | 0 | 0.5 | 0 | 0.05 | 0.5 | none configured |
| 1 | robot1_front_right_thigh_joint | rad | [-inf, +inf] | 0 | 0.5 | 0 | 0.05 | 0.5 | none configured |
| 2 | robot1_rl_wheel_joint | rad/s | [-inf, +inf] | 0 | 5 | 0 | 0.5 | 5 | none configured |
| 3 | robot1_rr_wheel_joint | rad/s | [-inf, +inf] | 0 | 5 | 0 | 0.5 | 5 | none configured |
For a negative change du, the unclipped target changes by scale*du. A zero position input requests the listed offset, not the current measured joint angle. A zero-offset velocity input requests zero speed, not instantaneous braking.
For position channels, radians convert to degrees by *57.2957795: 0.025 rad is about 1.43 degrees; 0.05 rad about 2.86 degrees.

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 20 simulated seconds, at 50 control Hz (1000 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
For the final 10 seconds, maintain rear-wheel-only support, front wheels clear of the ground, base height within 0.08 m of 0.828 m and upright tilt <=15 degrees.
Keep root horizontal position within 0.75 m of reset. Original horizontal velocity pushes every 10-15 s remain enabled.

Flamingo / wheel_legged_jump_balance

Flamingo完成两次跳跃，落地站稳

2条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（43步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让Flamingo跟踪运动/高度命令，并在原生jump event激活时起跳、落地并保持平衡。

新场景：按两次指令跳起，然后落地保持平衡。

环境设置

  • 在3s、10s发送跳跃指令，期间保持原地目标；保留13–15s原速度推扰及幅度配置。
  • 每条指令开启一次跳跃机会；机体上升≥8cm且双轮离地后，再双轮接地，记一次完成。每条指令最多记一次。
  • 保留原base/hip/shoulder/leg接触失败。

预算：1000步 / 50Hz ≈ 20s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 最终状态 · 评测结束时

    两次指令对应的跳跃均已完成。

    completed_jumps == 2
  2. 连续保持 · 最后 2 秒

    落地平衡在最后连续保持2秒。

    landed_stable == true
状态怎么计算？
  • completed_jumps：由真实高度/接触状态的“起跳→落地”转换累计，不由模型报数。
  • landed_stable：双轮支撑、机体倾角≤15°且|竖直速度|≤0.15m/s。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：删除多层起跳/落地限时，保留真实跳跃完成计数与末段落稳。

当前源码任务说明摘录；未找到该题outputs运行快照，未伪造完整运行时prompt/关节表

原文件
Control the original Flamingo rev01_5_2 wheel-legged robot to follow native velocity commands, jump when its native event command is active, land and continue balancing. No pretrained jump/gait policy is supplied. Native low-level delayed PD/gear actuator remains; it does not choose the whole-body jump or balance sequence.
There are exactly8native actions in this order: [left_hip,right_hip,left_shoulder,right_shoulder,left_leg,right_leg,left_wheel,right_wheel]. The first6are absolute actuator position targets with scale1rad and no default offset. The final2are wheel velocity commands with scale40rad/s and no offset. Important: leg joints use the ORIGINAL GearDelayedPDActuator, gear_ratio=-1.5: measured physical leg position/velocity is multiplied by-1.5 before motor-space PD, and torque is mapped back by gear ratio. Thus leg target is motor-space; do not treat it as direct physical leg-joint angle. Action term does not declare an explicit clip; motor/joint limits remain. Delays randomly span0-4physical ticks (0-20ms); keep feedback stable.
The allowed native actor groups are stack_policy and none_stack_policy, never their critic counterparts. stack_policy is28values: hip+shoulder positions4, leg motor-space positions2, hip+shoulder velocities4(scale.15), leg motor-space velocities2(scale.15), wheel velocities2(scale.15), base angular velocity3(scale.25), projected gravity3, previous action8. Within each regex-resolved term use runtime metadata for joint order. Original noise remains active. none_stack_policy contains velocity_commands4 (vx,vy,yaw,height; first3 scaled[1,0,.25],height unscaled) and event_commands2(active flag,elapsed time). For TrackJump base linear velocity,base height,height scan,current reward,contact flag,lift mask,roll/pitch commands are removed from actor.
The API provides actor_vector using the ORIGINAL co_rl StateHandler with current+2previous stack_policy frames (newest first), followed by current none_stack_policy: typically90values. Initial history repeats the first frame, exactly the native handler. It additionally labels stack_policy_latest and none_stack_policy_current; they contain no new privileged data. Native config class default stacks0 differs from author train/play CLI default2; this adapter explicitly uses CLI default2 and records it.
Native physics200Hz, control50Hz (.02s/action), horizon20s=1000steps. Jump event resamples3-5s, lasts1.2s, first activates only after native2s warmup;10% standing events suppress jumps. Native reward emphasizes upward velocity/ground push during event elapsed0.3-0.8s, wheel quietness in event, orientation, smoothness and command tracking. Velocity command resamples9-13s; vx[-1.5,1.5]m/s,yaw[-2.5,2.5]rad/s. Preserve these commands rather than overwriting them.
A native velocity push arrives13-15s: x[-1.5,1.5],y[-1,1],z[-1,.5]m/s. Mass/friction/reset perturbations stay enabled. Base,hip,shoulder,leg unwanted contacts can terminate; this is NOT Play, whose push magnitude is zero. There is no upstream full jump+stable-landing binary success; evaluator reports original reward terms/termination and event command traces, not survival-as-success.
For quick takeoff/landing/balance feedback consider coding_control if enabled: read only provided actor data each control tick and return the same8Daction. Code cannot query simulator truth or modify scene, command,physics,reset or scoring. Continue until native termination or budget. Third-person review rendering is for the human only.

当前英文任务附加段 · World v1已启用

World evaluation profile world-state-v1 (World-authored task; not official binary SR).
The action contract and actor observation restrictions above remain binding.
The task objective, timing and disturbances in this section replace the original random-command objective.
Evaluation lasts 20 simulated seconds, at 50 control Hz (1000 actions maximum).
All listed conditions must pass together. Native failure or scene boundary violation overrides success.
A short run, missing measurements, or claiming completion cannot establish success.
At evaluation end, completed_jumps must equal 2; each commanded jump counts only after actual airborne rise of at least 0.08 m followed by landing.
For the final 2 seconds, maintain two-wheel ground support, tilt <=15 degrees and absolute vertical speed <=0.15 m/s.
Remain in place. Two native event commands activate at t=3 and 10 s, each lasting 1.2 s; they replace random jump-command timing. Native velocity pushes remain. A jump counts once per command only after both wheels leave support, body rises at least 0.08 m, and both wheels regain support. Raw actor history and joint motor targets remain unchanged.
沿用的英文动作接口说明

已通过构造状态正反例与真实环境状态读取；仍未证明每项场景均有物理成功轨迹，尤其always条件的可行性与难度仍待参考控制验证。

查看原生评分卡片（保留作对照）
WheeledLab / mushr-drift

MuSHR受扰漂移，完成一圈

2条状态检查设计已认可＋

实现验证：构造状态正反例通过；Docker状态读取通过（400步诊断）。尚无该题物理成功轨迹证明。 原始判定记录 · 验证报告及视频

原场景：让后驱MuSHR在原生环形/直线组合赛道范围内高速漂移，以速度、侧滑、轨迹偏差和进展等连续奖励评价；原任务没有“完成一圈即成功”的规则。

新场景：沿固定赛道完成一圈，在两处弯道维持漂移。

环境设置

  • 原stadium赛道加可见线，顺序固定的4个门防止抄近道/反复刷起点；World通道宽1m。
  • 保留原0.1–0.4s速度/角速扰动、0.8–1.2s较大yaw扰动以及摩擦/增益随机化；参数/seed在环境配置中冻结。
  • 保留原越界终止；完整车辆安全外框越出World通道为场景失败。

预算：400步 / 50Hz ≈ 8s。
结束：固定模拟时长结束时T；不提前成功退出。

成功判定 · 以下状态全部满足

  1. 最终状态 · 评测结束时

    规定路线已完成一圈。

    lap_complete == true
  2. 最终状态 · 评测结束时

    两处弯道均已完成持续漂移。

    drift_corners >= 2
状态怎么计算？
  • lap_complete：按正向依次经过4门并重新越过起终线；按位姿轨迹计算。
  • drift_corners：不同弯道内满足前向vx≥1m/s且0.25≤|atan2(vy,vx)|≤0.55rad连续0.30s的弯道数量。

过程状态只由评测端真实轨迹累计，不额外发给模型；保持时间使用模拟时间。

这次简化：去掉平均速度1.5m/s和扰动计数；仅看整圈状态及两个弯道的漂移完成状态。

原状态或奖励有依据，不代表新增容差和时长已经验证；所有新阈值仍需校准。

    原progress不是圈数；有序圈数为World新状态。

    原判定映射：O02

    mushr_drift_env_cfg.py:160–165

    完整源文件

    160: def track_progress_rate(env):
161:     '''Estimate track progress by positive z-axis angular velocity around the environment'''
162:     asset : RigidObject = env.scene[SceneEntityCfg("robot").name]
163:     root_ang_vel = asset.data.root_link_ang_vel_w # this is different than the mdp one
164:     progress_rate = root_ang_vel[..., 2]
165:     return progress_rate
  2. S02 · 两处弯道均已完成持续漂移。

    侧滑区间/速度门槛有原reward依据；逐弯0.30秒和两个弯新增。

    原判定映射：O01

    mushr_drift_env_cfg.py:30–31

    完整源文件

    30: STRAIGHT = 0.8                # Shaping
31: SLIP_THRESHOLD = 0.55         # (rad) For reward
    mushr_drift_env_cfg.py:219–230

    完整源文件

    219: def side_slip(env, min_thresh: float, max_thresh: float, min_vel_x: float=0.5):
220:     vel = mdp.base_lin_vel(env)
221:     slip_angle = torch.abs(torch.atan2(vel[...,1], vel[...,0]))
222:     valid_angle = torch.where(torch.logical_or(
223:         torch.abs(vel[..., 0]) < min_vel_x, slip_angle > max_thresh),
224:         0.0, slip_angle
225:     )
226:     # Discount lateral vel from steering
227:     valid_angle = torch.where(valid_angle < min_thresh, 0.0, valid_angle)
228:     # Clamp unstable angles. Harder than zeroing for heavy, unstable vehicles
229:     # valid_angle = torch.clamp(valid_angle, max=max_thresh)
230:     return valid_angle
    mushr_drift_env_cfg.py:246–254

    完整源文件

    246:     side_slip = RewTerm(
247:         func=side_slip,
248:         weight=10., # Increases over time
249:         params={
250:             "min_thresh": 0.25, # 15 degrees
251:             "max_thresh": SLIP_THRESHOLD,
252:             "min_vel_x": 1.0
253:         }
254:     )
